- d5: queen.
- e6: must be covered. Who can cover e6? Bishop from c4 covers e6. Also queen on d5 covers e6.
- f5: covered by rook f1.
- f4: covered by rook f1.
- e4: covered by queen on d5.
- d4: covered by queen on d5.
- d6: covered by queen on d5.
- f6: covered by queen on d5? d5 to f6 is diagonal? Yes, queen on d5 covers f6 (d5-e6-f6). So queen on d5 covers all those squares except f5 and f4, which are covered by the rook. Bxc4 followed by Qd5#. And it's unstoppable because Black has no checks and no way to disrupt it.
